{"forms":[{"form":"lobsters","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A crustacean of the Nephropidae family, dark green or blue-black in colour turning bright red when cooked, with a hard shell and claws, which is used as a seafood."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Various other crustaceans that resemble true lobsters:","A spiny lobster, also called the rock lobster, a crustacean of the Palinuridae family, pinkish red in colour, with a hard, spiny shell but no claws, which is used as a seafood."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Various other crustaceans that resemble true lobsters:","A slipper lobster (a crustacean of the family Scyllaridae)."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Various other crustaceans that resemble true lobsters:","A squat lobster."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A soldier or officer of the imperial British Army (due to their red or scarlet uniform)."],"tags":["countable","historical","slang","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["An Australian twenty-dollar note, due to its reddish-orange colour."],"tags":["countable","slang","uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈlɒb.stə/","tags":["Northern-England","Received-Pronunciation","Wales"]},{"ipa":"/ˈlɔb.stə/","tags":["Australia","British","Southern","Standard"]},{"ipa":"/ˈlɑb.stɚ/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈlɒb.stɚ/","tags":["Canada"]},{"ipa":"/ˈlɔb.stəɾ/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"ipa":"/ˈlɒb.stə/","tags":["New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"[ˈlɔ̟b.stə] ~ [ˈlɞ̠b.stə]","tags":["New-Zealand"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"crustacean","word":"sea locust"},{"sense":"British soldier","word":"lobsterback"},{"sense":"British soldier","word":"redcoat"}],"upstream_line":208740,"upstream_offset":1005780663,"upstream_record_sha256":"663b7748448e7b4ec27302a95d2d92c876a89fe8cb505c554f1b6c20a3e1139d","word":"lobster"}
